PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, airplane_1 is at location_1_0, package_0 is at location_1_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0 and location_1_0 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_0_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, airplane_1 is at location_1_0, package_0 is at location_1_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0 and location_1_0 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_0_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain logistics_domain)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types
    city location locatable - object
    package vehicle - locatable
    truck airplane - vehicle
  )

  (:predicates
    (at ?obj - locatable ?loc - location)
    (in_truck ?p - package ?t - truck)
    (in_airplane ?p - package ?a - airplane)
    (in_city ?l - location ?c - city)
    (is_airport ?l - location)
  )

  (:action load_truck
    :parameters (?p - package ?t - truck ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (at ?p ?l) (at ?t ?l))
    :effect (and (not (at ?p ?l)) (in_truck ?p ?t))
  )

  (:action unload_truck
    :parameters (?p - package ?t - truck ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (in_truck ?p ?t) (at ?t ?l))
    :effect (and (at ?p ?l) (not (in_truck ?p ?t)))
  )

  (:action load_airplane
    :parameters (?p - package ?a - airplane ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (at ?p ?l) (at ?a ?l))
    :effect (and (not (at ?p ?l)) (in_airplane ?p ?a))
  )

  (:action unload_airplane
    :parameters (?p - package ?a - airplane ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (in_airplane ?p ?a) (at ?a ?l))
    :effect (and (at ?p ?l) (not (in_airplane ?p ?a)))
  )

  (:action drive_truck
    :parameters (?t - truck ?from - location ?to - location ?c - city)
    :precondition (and (at ?t ?from) (in_city ?from ?c) (in_city ?to ?c))
    :effect (and (not (at ?t ?from)) (at ?t ?to))
  )

  (:action fly_airplane
    :parameters (?a - airplane ?from - location ?to - location)
    :precondition (and (at ?a ?from) (is_airport ?from) (is_airport ?to))
    :effect (and (not (at ?a ?from)) (at ?a ?to))
  )
)
</domain>

<problem>
(define (problem transport_package_0)
  (:domain logistics_domain)
  (:objects
    city_0 city_1 - city
    location_0_0 location_1_0 - location
    truck_0 truck_1 - truck
    airplane_0 airplane_1 - airplane
    package_0 - package
  )

  (:init
    ;; Aircraft initial positions
    (at airplane_0 location_1_0)
    (at airplane_1 location_1_0)
    
    ;; Truck initial positions
    (at truck_0 location_0_0)
    (at truck_1 location_1_0)
    
    ;; Package initial positions
    (at package_0 location_1_0)
    
    ;; City mappings
    (in_city location_0_0 city_0)
    (in_city location_1_0 city_1)
    
    ;; Airport definitions
    (is_airport location_0_0)
    (is_airport location_1_0)
  )

  (:goal
    (at package_0 location_0_0)
  )
)
</problem>
================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain logistics_domain)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types
    city location locatable - object
    package vehicle - locatable
    truck airplane - vehicle
  )

  (:predicates
    (at ?obj - locatable ?loc - location)
    (in_truck ?p - package ?t - truck)
    (in_airplane ?p - package ?a - airplane)
    (in_city ?l - location ?c - city)
    (is_airport ?l - location)
  )

  (:action load_truck
    :parameters (?p - package ?t - truck ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (at ?p ?l) (at ?t ?l))
    :effect (and (not (at ?p ?l)) (in_truck ?p ?t))
  )

  (:action unload_truck
    :parameters (?p - package ?t - truck ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (in_truck ?p ?t) (at ?t ?l))
    :effect (and (at ?p ?l) (not (in_truck ?p ?t)))
  )

  (:action load_airplane
    :parameters (?p - package ?a - airplane ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (at ?p ?l) (at ?a ?l))
    :effect (and (not (at ?p ?l)) (in_airplane ?p ?a))
  )

  (:action unload_airplane
    :parameters (?p - package ?a - airplane ?l - location)
    :precondition (and (in_airplane ?p ?a) (at ?a ?l))
    :effect (and (at ?p ?l) (not (in_airplane ?p ?a)))
  )

  (:action drive_truck
    :parameters (?t - truck ?from - location ?to - location ?c - city)
    :precondition (and (at ?t ?from) (in_city ?from ?c) (in_city ?to ?c))
    :effect (and (not (at ?t ?from)) (at ?t ?to))
  )

  (:action fly_airplane
    :parameters (?a - airplane ?from - location ?to - location)
    :precondition (and (at ?a ?from) (is_airport ?from) (is_airport ?to))
    :effect (and (not (at ?a ?from)) (at ?a ?to))
  )
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
To transport **package_0** from its starting point at **location_1_0** (the airport in city_1) to its destination at **location_0_0** (the airport in city_0), the following plan has been established:

**Step 1: Load the package onto the aircraft**
At timestamp **T=0**, load **package_0** into **airplane_1** at **location_1_0**. Both the package and the airplane are currently at this location. This action takes 1 unit of time.

**Step 2: Fly to the destination city**
At timestamp **T=1**, fly **airplane_1** from the airport at **location_1_0** to the airport at **location_0_0**. This completes the transfer between city_1 and city_0. This action takes 1 unit of time.

**Step 3: Unload the package**
At timestamp **T=2**, unload **package_0** from **airplane_1** at **location_0_0**. This action takes 1 unit of time.

**Final Result:**
The package is now at **location_0_0**, satisfying the goal. 
**Total Plan Duration:** 3 units of time.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, package_0 is at location_1_0, package_1 is at location_0_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0 and location_1_0 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_1_0 and package_1 is at location_1_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To transport the packages to their required destinations, follow these instructions:

**Step 1: Fly airplane_0 from location_1_0 to location_0_0**
- **Action**: Fly the airplane (airplane_0) from its current location at the airport in city_1 (location_1_0) to the airport in city_0 (location_0_0).
- **Time/Timestamp**: 0 to 1 hour (Unit Time 1)

**Step 2: Load package_1 into airplane_0 at location_0_0**
- **Action**: At the airport in city_0 (location_0_0), load the target package (package_1) onto the airplane (airplane_0).
- **Time/Timestamp**: 1 to 2 hours (Unit Time 2)

**Step 3: Fly airplane_0 from location_0_0 to location_1_0**
- **Action**: Pilot the airplane (airplane_0) carrying the package from the airport in city_0 (location_0_0) back to the airport in city_1 (location_1_0).
- **Time/Timestamp**: 2 to 3 hours (Unit Time 3)

**Step 4: Unload package_1 from airplane_0 at location_1_0**
- **Action**: Once the airplane (airplane_0) has landed at the airport in city_1 (location_1_0), unload the package (package_1) so that it is stationed at the location.
- **Time/Timestamp**: 3 to 4 hours (Unit Time 4)

**Final Status**: 
- package_0 remains at its starting position (location_1_0).
- package_1 has been successfully delivered to location_1_0.
- All goal conditions are met.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, package_0 is at location_1_0, package_1 is at location_1_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0 and location_1_0 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_0_0 and package_1 is at location_0_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To successfully transport the packages as required, please follow these instructions:

**Step 1: Load Package 1**  
At **location_1_0**, load **package_1** into **airplane_0**. Ensure the airplane and the package are both at this location before proceeding.

**Step 2: Load Package 0**  
Still at **location_1_0**, load **package_0** into **airplane_0**. Both packages should now be secured inside the airplane.

**Step 3: Fly to Destination**  
Fly **airplane_0** from the airport at **location_1_0** (City 1) to the airport at **location_0_0** (City 0). 

**Step 4: Unload Package 1**  
Upon arrival at **location_0_0**, unload **package_1** from **airplane_0**. The package should now be positioned at the airport in City 0.

**Step 5: Unload Package 0**  
Finally, unload **package_0** from **airplane_0** at **location_0_0**. 

**Goal Status:** Both packages are now successfully delivered to location_0_0.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_0_0, airplane_1 is at location_0_0, package_0 is at location_1_0, package_1 is at location_1_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0 and location_1_0 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_1_0 and package_1 is at location_0_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL domain, problem, and plan, here are the natural language instructions to transport the packages as required.

### Logistics Plan Execution

**Initial State:**
*   **airplane_1** is at **location_0_0** (Airport, City 0).
*   **package_1** is at **location_1_0** (Airport, City 1).
*   **package_0** is already at its goal destination, **location_1_0**.

**Step 1: Relocate transport aircraft**
*   **Action:** Fly **airplane_1** from **location_0_0** to **location_1_0**.
*   **Timestamp:** 00:00 - 01:00
*   **Description:** The aircraft moves from the airport in city_0 to the airport in city_1 to prepare for package pickup.

**Step 2: Load cargo**
*   **Action:** Load **package_1** into **airplane_1** at **location_1_0**.
*   **Timestamp:** 01:00 - 01:15
*   **Description:** Secure package_1 inside the aircraft while both are positioned at the city_1 airport.

**Step 3: Transport cargo to destination city**
*   **Action:** Fly **airplane_1** from **location_1_0** to **location_0_0**.
*   **Timestamp:** 01:15 - 02:15
*   **Description:** Fly the aircraft containing the package back to the airport in city_0.

**Step 4: Unload cargo**
*   **Action:** Unload **package_1** from **airplane_1** at **location_0_0**.
*   **Timestamp:** 02:15 - 02:30
*   **Description:** Remove package_1 from the aircraft at the city_0 airport to complete the delivery.

**Final Goal Status:**
*   **package_0**: At location_1_0 (Goal met).
*   **package_1**: At location_0_0 (Goal met).

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, package_0 is at location_1_0, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_0, location_0_0 is in the city city_0, location_0_1 is in the city city_0, location_1_0 is in the city city_1 and location_1_1 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_1_1.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To deliver package_0 to its destination at location_1_1, the following sequence of actions must be performed. Each action is estimated to take one unit of time.

**Step 1: Load package_0**
- **Action:** Load package_0 into truck_1.
- **Location:** location_1_0 (City: city_1).
- **Time:** 00:00 - 00:01
- **Description:** At location_1_0, package_0 is loaded onto truck_1. Both the package and the truck are currently at this location.

**Step 2: Transport package_0**
- **Action:** Drive truck_1 from location_1_0 to location_1_1.
- **Location:** within city_1.
- **Time:** 00:01 - 00:02
- **Description:** truck_1, carrying package_0, is driven from its current location (location_1_0) to the destination location (location_1_1) within the same city (city_1).

**Step 3: Unload package_0**
- **Action:** Unload package_0 from truck_1.
- **Location:** location_1_1 (City: city_1).
- **Time:** 00:02 - 00:03
- **Description:** Upon arrival at location_1_1, package_0 is unloaded from truck_1. The package is now at its final destination, completing the task.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_0_0, package_0 is at location_0_1, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_1, location_0_0 is in the city city_0, location_0_1 is in the city city_0, location_1_0 is in the city city_1 and location_1_1 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_1_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================

To transport package_0 from its starting position at location_0_1 to the destination at location_1_0, the following sequence of actions will be performed:

**Step 1: Drive truck_0 to the package**

- **Time:** 00:00 - 00:01

**Step 2: Load the package into the truck**
- **Action:** Load package_0 into truck_0 at location_0_1.
- **Time:** 00:01 - 00:02

**Step 3: Drive the package to the airport**

**Step 4: Unload the package at the airport**

- **Time:** 00:05 - 00:06

**Step 7: Unload the package at the destination**

- **Time:** 00:06 - 00:07

PROBLEM
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, airplane_1 is at location_0_0, package_0 is at location_1_1, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_1, location_0_0 is in the city city_0, location_0_1 is in the city city_0, location_1_0 is in the city city_1 and location_1_1 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_0_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
I have to plan logistics to transport packages within cities via trucks and between cities via airplanes. Locations within a city are directly connected (trucks can move between any two such locations), and so are the cities. In each city there is exactly one truck and each city has one location that serves as an airport.
Here are the actions that can be performed:

Load a package into a truck. For example, load package_1 into truck_1 at location_1_1.
Load a package into an airplane. For example, load package_1 into airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from a truck. For example, unload package_1 from truck_1 at location_1_1.
Unload a package from an airplane. For example, unload package_1 from airplane_1 at location_1_1.
Drive a truck from one location to another location. For example, drive truck_1 from location_1_1 to location_1_2 in city_1.
Fly an airplane from one city to another city. For example, fly airplane_1 from location_1_1 to location_2_1. Here location_1_1 is the airport in city_1 and location_2_1 is the airport in city_2.

The following are the restrictions on the actions:
A package can be loaded into a truck only if the package and the truck are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into a truck, the package is not at the location and is in the truck.   
A package can be loaded into an airplane only if the package and the airplane are in the same location.
Once a package is loaded into an airplane, the package is not at the location and is in the airplane.
A package can be unloaded from a truck only if the package is in the truck.
Once a package is unloaded from a truck, the package is not in the truck and is at the location of the truck.
A package can be unloaded from an airplane only if the package in the airplane.
Once a package is unloaded from an airplane, the package is not in the airplane and is at the location of the airplane.   
A truck can be driven from one location to another if the truck is at the from-location and both from-location and to-location are locations in the same city.
Once a truck is driven from one location to another, it is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.
An airplane can be flown from one city to another if the from-location and the to-location are airports and the airplane is at the from-location.
Once an airplane is flown from one city to another the airplane is not at the from-location and is at the to-location.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, location_0_0 is an airport, location_1_0 is an airport, airplane_0 is at location_1_0, airplane_1 is at location_0_0, package_0 is at location_1_1, truck_0 is at location_0_0, truck_1 is at location_1_1, location_0_0 is in the city city_0, location_0_1 is in the city city_0, location_1_0 is in the city city_1 and location_1_1 is in the city city_1.
My goal is to have that package_0 is at location_0_0.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]

2.  **08:05 AM - Drive Truck:**
    Drive **truck_1** from **location_1_1** to **location_1_0** within **city_1**.

3.  **08:15 AM - Unload Package:**
    Unload **package_0** from **truck_1** at **location_1_0** (the airport in city_1).

4.  **08:20 AM - Load Airplane:**

5.  **08:30 AM - Fly Airplane:**

6.  **09:15 AM - Final Unload:**
    Unload **package_0** from **airplane_0** at **location_0_0**.
